## Wulong Karst / Three Natural Bridges

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| Wulong Karst is part of the South China Karst World Heritage property (Phase I, 2007): "giant collapse depressions, called Tiankeng, and exceptionally high natural bridges between them, with long stretches of deep unroofed caves"; its landscapes hold evidence for the history of the Yangtze and its tributaries. Basis for why[1] ("the bridges are what is left of a cave"). | [UNESCO 1248](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1248/) |
| Three bridges, Tianlong, Qinglong and Heilong, cross the Yangshui River gorge in a line, "三桥夹两坑" (two sinkholes between three bridges); billed as Asia's largest natural-bridge group (not used). The site lies 20 km south-east of Wulong town, in the south of the Fairy Mountain area. | [Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism: 武隆天生三桥景区](https://www.cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/jdjd_1/202007/t20200702_7634529.html) |
| Tianlong: height 235 m, average arch height 96 m ("nearly 100 metres"). Two openings under it; the left (south) one's floor is 120 m higher than the right one; the underground river once flowed through the left and later shifted to the right. | Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism (above); [Wikipedia: Three Natural Bridges](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Three_Natural_Bridges) |
| Qinglong: 281 m, the tallest of the three; named because after rain a waterfall pours off the bridge into mist and, in sunshine, forms a rainbow, "似青龙扶摇直上". Looking up from below, the arch shows curved, parallel faces where the roof collapsed in stages. The source also calls it the tallest karst natural bridge in the world; that superlative is not used. | Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism (above) |
| Heilong: named for its deep, dark arch, "似有一条黑龙蜿蜒于洞顶"; four hanging springs on the north wall, 雾泉、珍珠泉、一线泉、三叠泉. The story translates the names and hedges the flow ("depends on recent rain"). | Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism (above) |
| From the top end of Tianlong to the bottom end of Heilong is 1,500 m in a straight line ("within a kilometre and a half"). The two sinkholes between the bridges are 276–285 m deep ("close to 300 metres"). The Yangshui is a tributary of the Wu River (乌江), which joins the Yangtze. | [Wikipedia: Three Natural Bridges](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Three_Natural_Bridges); [Chinese Wikipedia: 天生三桥](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E5%A4%A9%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%89%E6%A1%A5) |
| Sensory lines: "桥下溪流潺潺，喷泉叮咚", cliffs and green shrubs ("四周岩石峥嵘，灌木苍翠"); in the sinkhole "抬头仰望，四处是悬崖万丈，有山泉从上飞泻而下". | Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism (both pages); [Wulong Tourism Net (wlkst.com): 天生三桥旅游攻略](https://www.wlkst.com/lylx/20191211/22848.html) |
| The Tianlong lift: a cliffside glass sightseeing lift ("天龙旋梯"), vertical height 135.85 m, running height 115 m, in service from 31 December 2022; visitors reach it along a cliff walkway and a tunnel. The story says only "a glass lift slides down the cliff". The tour product names it as included. | [Wulong Tourism Net, 2022-12-28](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20221228/26282.html); `lib/privateTourProducts.ts` |
| Route: shuttle from the visitor centre, lift down, walk the gorge past Tianfu Post and the three bridges, then "途中有一段上坡路，也可以乘坐电瓶车到出口", and an eco-bus back to the visitor centre in town or on to Longshui Gorge (its own ticket). Route length: a travel agency gives about 5 km "从崖壁到谷底", so the story says "a few kilometres". Visit time: 2–3 hours (Wulong Tourism Net "2小时左右"; agencies 2–3 h). | [Wulong Tourism Net](https://www.wlkst.com/lylx/20191211/22848.html); [Chongqing Bendibao](http://cq.bendibao.com/tour/202017/80838.shtm) |
| Tianfu Post (天福官驿): grey-tiled courtyard in the sinkhole before Tianlong Bridge, about 20 rooms, lanterns; "完全按照唐朝房屋样式建造"; holds props from Curse of the Golden Flower; the film's "唯一的外景拍摄地点". The original post house dates from 619 and was destroyed in war; the present building was rebuilt for the film (Sina). One search summary gives 2005 for the rebuild; it could not be confirmed on a primary page, so the story gives the film's 2006 release instead of a build year. | [Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism: 细看天福官驿](https://cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/ywtj/202112/t20211210_10141671.html); [Wulong District (三桥 page)](https://www.cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/jdjd_1/202007/t20200702_7634529.html); [Sina, 2017](https://k.sina.cn/article_5901594174_15fc32e3e0010030ky.html) |
| Curse of the Golden Flower and Transformers 4 were filmed here (Transformers not used in the story). | [Chongqing government, 2025](https://www.cq.gov.cn/ywdt/zwhd/qxdt/202507/t20250703_14772857.html) |
| Wulong Karst: World Heritage June 2007 (27 June); the tourist area is the Three Natural Bridges, Fairy Mountain and Furong Cave; 5A since 6 July 2011. | [Chongqing government, 2026](https://www.cq.gov.cn/zjcq/cycq/zmjd/zqaaaaajjq/202606/t20260604_15729062.html) |
| Fairy Mountain: altitude 2,033 m, forest and natural grassland, summer average 22°C ("夏季平均气温22度"), "林海雪原" in winter. **(revision)** The Chongqing Civil Affairs Bureau (July 2026) gives the resort area (1,100–1,700 m) "夏季平均气温仅21℃" and calls it a 避暑 retreat "当重庆主城被热浪席卷". The story now says "21 to 22°C" (zh 二十一二度, ko 21~22℃), an average, not daytime highs, and "people from Chongqing come up here to escape the heat". iChongqing's 24°C is not used. | [Chongqing government, 2026](https://www.cq.gov.cn/zjcq/cycq/zmjd/zqaaaaajjq/202606/t20260604_15729062.html); [iChongqing](https://www.ichongqing.info/attraction/wulong-karst-national-geology-park/) |
| Furong Cave: main cave 2,700 m, visitor path 1,860 m, stable temperature deep inside 16.1°C, beside the Furong River (芙蓉江畔); formations such as 珊瑚瑶池 and 石花之王 ("glittering formations"). | [Chongqing government, 2026](https://www.cq.gov.cn/zjcq/cycq/zmjd/zqaaaaajjq/202606/t20260604_15729062.html); [Chongqing government, 2025](https://www.cq.gov.cn/ywdt/zwhd/qxdt/202507/t20250703_14772857.html) |
| The bridges' visitor centre is in Xiannü (Fairy Mountain) town; shuttles leave from there. | [Wulong Tourism Net](https://www.wlkst.com/lylx/20191211/22848.html); [iChongqing](https://www.ichongqing.info/attraction/wulong-karst-national-geology-park/) |
| Road time: Chongqing to Wulong town about 2 h on the G65, then about 0.5 h to the Xiannü town visitor centre ("two and a half to three hours each way"). The Wulong government page gives 140 km on the expressway plus about 10 km. | [Wulong Tourism Net](https://www.wlkst.com/lylx/20191211/22848.html); Wulong District (三桥 page) |
| High-speed rail: Chongqing East to Wulong South opened 27 June 2025, about 34 minutes ("about half an hour"). The story does not name the station; the city guide's warning that the station is not the bridges' entrance is kept ("still a road transfer"). | [Chongqing Bendibao, 2025](http://cq.bendibao.com/traffic/2025626/154859.shtm); [Wulong Tourism Net, 2025](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20250626/27424.html); `content/destinations/chongqing/body.en.ts` |
| National Day crowds: "国庆假期 … 天生三桥游人如织". The story says "at its most crowded" and does not quantify. | [Wulong Tourism Net, 2021](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20211002/25589.html); [Wulong Tourism Net, 2021](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20211009/25624.html) |
| Wikidata: Q7797661 (Three Natural Bridges; en/zh/ko sitelinks). Wulong Karst is Q8039213; sameAs uses the bridges, which is what the page is about, plus the UNESCO property. | [Wikidata Q7797661](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q7797661) |
| Korean names: 우롱 (style guide), 천생삼교 (Korean Wikipedia, MyRealTrip, Tourvis), 천갱, 천복관역, 선녀산 (Trip.com KR also 셴뉘산), 부용동, 용수협 (Tourvis "용수협곡"), 천룡교·청룡교·흑룡교. 乌江 is 우장강 (Korean Wikipedia redirect to 오강 (강)). Films: 황후화 and 트랜스포머: 사라진 시대 (Korean Wikipedia). | [Korean Wikipedia: 천생삼교](https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EC%B2%9C%EC%83%9D%EC%82%BC%EA%B5%90); [Trip.com KR](https://kr.trip.com/moments/theme/poi-tianlong-sky-hole-56809976-attraction-993137/); [Tourvis](https://tourvis.com/activity/product/PRD3009850035) |
| **(revision)** The lift turns: "天龙旋梯…为旋转双层轿厢，增加了游客鸟瞰、俯视、远眺的功能，站在电梯内可以从不同角度观赏"; "透明电梯". The story says "a glass lift turns as it slides down the cliff" (zh 一边降一边转, ko 회전하며). The reviewer's "slowly" is not used: no source gives the turning speed. | [Wulong Tourism Net, 2022-12-28](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20221228/26282.html) |
| **(revision)** Heavy rain closes the gorge at short notice. 7 June 2026: "受持续强降雨天气影响，天生三桥、龙水峡地缝道路被洪水淹没…暂停营业，恢复时间另行通知"; the bridges reopened on 8 June. 12 July 2024: "受暴雨天气影响，天生三桥景区和龙水峡地缝景区临时闭园". The story says "heavy rain can close the gorge at short notice", not "for a day", because the 2024 notice gave no reopening date. | [Wulong Tourism Net notice, 2026-06-07](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/27873.jhtml); [Chongqing government, 2026-06-08](https://www.cq.gov.cn/ywdt/bmts/202606/t20260608_15737519.html); [Wulong Tourism Net notice, 2024-07-12](https://www.wlkst.com/wlxw/20240712/26973.html) |
| **(revision)** Tianfu Post yard: "院内还有一辆供官员出行的马车…《满城尽带黄金甲》剧组拍摄时所用到的道具"; "完全按照唐朝房屋样式建造". The story says "an official's horse carriage and props from the shoot". The reviewer's "wooden" carriage is not in the source and is not used. "唯一的外景拍摄地点" re-checked on the Three Bridges page. | [Wulong District Commission of Culture and Tourism: 细看天福官驿](https://cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/ywtj/202112/t20211210_10141671.html); [Wulong District (三桥 page)](https://www.cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/jdjd_1/202007/t20200702_7634529.html) |
| **(revision)** Qinglong's name is hedged ("is said to come from", zh 据说, ko 왔다고 합니다): one official page gives the rainbow-dragon origin as fact, the other says "关于它的得名历来说法不一，其中之一据说是…". | [Wulong District (三桥 page)](https://www.cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/jdjd_1/202007/t20200702_7634529.html) |
| **(revision)** Tianlong's two openings: the source's 左(南)/右(北) is a fixed bearing, so zh and ko now say "high" and "low" (一高一低; 높은 쪽/낮은 쪽), as en does. Heilong's four springs are on "洞壁北侧"; "north" was dropped in all languages because visitors cannot tell which way is north under the arch. "The stream at your feet is that same river": the underground river "曾经从左穿洞流过，后来…改道为右侧的穿洞", i.e. the water that flows through the lower opening today. The story no longer says the river carved the cave. | [Wulong District (三桥 page)](https://www.cqwl.gov.cn/bmjz_sites/bm/wlw/zwxx_98939/jqjd/jdjd_1/202007/t20200702_7634529.html) |
| **(revision)** Furong Cave: the story now gives the 1,860 m visitor path ("nearly two kilometres"), not the 2,700 m main cave. "Stone curtains and glittering crystals" rests on the named formations "巨幕飞瀑" and "犬牙晶花" (with "石花之王", "珊瑚瑶池"). | [Chongqing government, 2026](https://www.cq.gov.cn/zjcq/cycq/zmjd/zqaaaaajjq/202606/t20260604_15729062.html); [Chongqing government, 2025](https://www.cq.gov.cn/ywdt/zwhd/qxdt/202507/t20250703_14772857.html) |
| **(revision)** "Fairy Mountain resort town": 仙女山镇 was replaced by 仙女山街道 on 22 April 2020, so en says "the Fairy Mountain resort town" and zh says "仙女山度假区" (仙女山国家级旅游度假区) in `pair`, not 仙女山镇. | [Chongqing Civil Affairs Bureau: 武隆仙女山 (2026)](https://mzj.cq.gov.cn/sy_218/bmdt/mzyw/202607/t20260713_15817014.html) |

## Dazu Rock Carvings

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| World Heritage 1999. Five clusters carved from the 9th to the 13th century, on steep hillsides; known for "the light that they shed on everyday life" and the synthesis of Buddhism, Taoism and Confucianism. Beishan: cliff 7–10 m high, about 300 m long, over 10,000 carvings, late 9th to mid-12th century. Baodingshan: a U-shaped gorge, carvings from the late 12th to the mid-13th century; the large group stretches about 500 m in 31 groups, with scenes of herdsmen and ordinary life. | [UNESCO 912](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/912/) |
| Baodingshan's Dafowan is a horseshoe-shaped valley; on 500-odd metres of cliff nearly 10,000 figures are carved "如同连环画", "题材无一重复", words and pictures together. Directed by Zhao Zhifeng over more than 70 years. | [Dazu District government: 赵智凤：清苦70载营建宝顶山石刻](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434462.html); [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html) |
| Carved 1174–1252 (南宋淳熙至淳祐年间), so "from the 1170s". Zhao Zhifeng was born in 1159 in Dazu. | [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html); [Xinhua, 2021](https://www.xinhuanet.com/2021-04/12/c_1127321754.htm) ("1174年前后") |
| Thousand-Hand Guanyin: niche 7.2 m high and 12.5 m wide, 88 m², carved, gilded and painted; hands "或五指平伸，或轻拈法器"; "宛如孔雀开屏" (official). The restoration (2008–2015, reopened 13 June 2015, about 440,000 sheets of gold leaf) counted the hands for the first time: 830. | [Xinhua, 2021](https://www.xinhuanet.com/2021-04/12/c_1127321754.htm); [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html) |
| Reclining Buddha: 31.6 m (31 m in other official pages), lying on his right side, eyes half closed, the legs below the knee "没入岩石之中"; the largest figure at Dazu. The 1945 Dazu survey team: "头大如屋" ("his head was as big as a house"). The folk saying about his hands and feet reaching other counties is not used. | [Dazu District government: 释迦涅槃](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202303/t20230321_11791450.html); [Chongqing Civil Affairs Bureau: 宝顶山](http://mzj.cq.gov.cn/sy_218/bmdt/mzyw/202412/t20241202_13847754.html) |
| Nine Dragons Bathing the Prince: nine dragon heads above; spring water flows "终年不息" from the central dragon's mouth onto the infant Buddha, seated with palms together. The rock face was where the hill's rainwater drained; the carvers stored it in a pool above the reclining Buddha and led it through a hidden channel to the dragon's mouth; it then runs into the zigzag "九曲黄河" channel in front of the reclining Buddha. | [Dazu District government: 释迦涅槃](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202303/t20230321_11791450.html) |
| Huayan Three Saints: about 7 m high; Manjushri holds a seven-storey stone pagoda 1.8 m high, over 800 jin (over 400 kg), "历八百余年而不坠". The story says "in his hands" (whether one hand or two was not checked). | [Dazu District government: 赵智凤](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434462.html) |
| Yuanjue Cave: 12 m deep; three Buddhas on the main wall, a kneeling figure before them, six seated figures on each side wall; robes "如丝似绸"; a skylight above the entrance sends light "直射窟心"; a dragon carved high on the right wall drips rainwater into a bowl, which drains through a hidden channel and a floor gutter. "Head bowed, palms together" is from Baidu Baike (俯首合十). "Largest cave in the valley" (Baidu) was dropped as unconfirmed on an official page. | [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html); [Dazu District government: 赵智凤](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434462.html) ("进深12米"); [Baidu Baike: 圆觉洞](https://baike.baidu.com/item/%E5%9C%86%E8%A7%89%E6%B4%9E/8500074) |
| Everyday scenes: parents raising children (父母恩重经变相, ten scenes); the hen woman: "养鸡女掀开鸡笼，鸡群争先恐后跑出，其中两只鸡在笼边争食蚯蚓"; drunk men and women; herd boys and oxen (12 ox-herding scenes along the rock). Inscription "三千条律令，不孝罪为先". A curly-haired figure in a monk's robe recurs in several niches, and scholars mostly identify him as Zhao Zhifeng ("学界多将其认为赵智凤的形象"). | [Dazu District government: 赵智凤](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434462.html); [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html) |
| Beishan: begun in 892 by Wei Junjing, carved over 250-odd years to the Shaoxing era; 1 km north of Dazu town (Chongqing culture and tourism; Dazu government says "about 2 km" on foot from the old crossroads, so the story says "just north of town"); called "中国观音造像的陈列馆". Cave 136 (转轮经藏窟), 1142–1146: a revolving sutra repository in the centre with a coiled dragon at its waist, and three figures on each side wall. Niche 125 (数珠手观音) is a Song masterpiece. | [Chongqing Municipal Commission of Culture and Tourism Development: 北山摩崖造像](https://whlyw.cq.gov.cn/zjwl/yzq/cqwlzy/zqwwzy/202405/t20240507_13182671.html); [Dazu District government: 震撼世界的大足石刻艺术](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434561.html) |
| Niche 125: hands crossed at the waist holding a string of beads, thin robe, ribbons fluttering ("两手交于腹际握一串数珠，衣薄透体，饰带迎风飘舞"). The 92 cm height appears only on secondary pages, so the story says "small". | [The Paper: 大足石刻旧影](https://www.thepaper.cn/newsDetail_forward_18705317) |
| Baodingshan is about 15 km north-east of Dazu town; Beishan is just north of town, so "20 to 30 minutes apart by car" (China Highlights gives 10 km, 20 minutes). The Dazu Rock Carvings Museum is in the Baodingshan scenic area. Baodingshan and Beishan sell separate tickets. Visit times: Baodingshan "全程预计4小时" including museum and cinema, Beishan about 2 hours; Trip.com KR about 2 hours for Baodingshan; the story gives 2–3 h for the Baodingshan carvings and 1–2 h for Beishan. | [Chongqing Civil Affairs Bureau: 宝顶山](http://mzj.cq.gov.cn/sy_218/bmdt/mzyw/202412/t20241202_13847754.html); [Chongqing Bendibao, 2024](https://cq.bendibao.com/tour/2024822/146324.shtm); [China Highlights](https://www.chinahighlights.com/chongqing/attraction/dazu-grottoes.htm) |
| About two hours each way by road from central Chongqing (China Highlights: about 100 km, 2-hour drive; iChongqing gives 2.5–3 h by bus). | [China Highlights](https://www.chinahighlights.com/chongqing/attraction/dazu-grottoes.htm); [iChongqing](https://www.ichongqing.info/attraction/dazu-rock-carvings/) |
| Leshan comparison: 71 m, a day out from Chengdu (from the Chengdu batch and the sight card). | [UNESCO 779](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/779/); `lib/sightsI18n.ts` |

The 400 kg and "800 years" were cut. Order along the cliff: Three Saints No. 5, Thousand-Hand Guanyin No. 8, reclining Buddha No. 11, so why[0] now walks them in that order. | [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html); [Dazu District government: 赵智凤](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202202/t20220225_10434462.html) |

| **(revision)** Yuanjue Cave drip: "幽静的圆觉洞内，岩石间时常有渗水，匠师在崖壁上雕凿一长龙…龙口下方站立一位形象古朴的老僧，中托起一钵，水经龙口下滴至钵中，又沿暗道流出洞外，寂静之际，可听见从龙口滴答的清脆之声". The story says "you may hear a drip". The draft's "look high on the right wall" was dropped: the 大足学 page's "右壁" may be counted from the Buddhas' side, not the visitor's, as it is for cave 136 below. | [Dazu District government: 下窥云雾隐神龙——大足唐宋石刻龙形象 (2024)](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/202402/t20240227_12965066.html); [Dazu District government: 大足学](https://www.dazu.gov.cn/rsdz/dzwh/dzsk/dzx/201809/t20180919_6856201.html) |

Begun 892 against Baodingshan's 1174, so "begun nearly three centuries earlier" (282 years); the dates themselves were cut from the page. Height given there as 1.08 m (The Paper and others give 92 cm), so the story keeps "small". | [CPPCC News (人民政协网), 2019: 时代审美的理想之像大足北山石刻造像](http://www.rmzxw.com.cn/c/2019-08-14/2408154.shtml) |
| **(revision)** Cave 136 light: Li Fangyin, head of the Dazu Academy, on CCTV: "整个洞窟面向西面，当下午阳光斜照到洞窟，并通过转轮藏映照到造像身上，进入洞窟的时候就有步移影换之妙". The story says "On a sunny afternoon, light slants in past the stone scripture case at its centre, and the shadows on the figures shift as you move." The reviewer's Samantabhadra-on-the-elephant line was not used: the only sources for its half-smile are a temple website and a blog, and they put him on the "右壁", while the official page puts him on the north wall; with the cave facing west, the north wall is on a visitor's left. The cave's central dragon ("须弥山中部束腰位置刻一蟠龙") was cut for length. The zh/ko draft's "standing" side figures were removed with it. | [West China City Daily, 2022](https://www.wccdaily.com.cn/wapepaper/html/20220427/173009.html); [Chongqing culture commission: 北山摩崖造像](https://whlyw.cq.gov.cn/zjwl/yzq/cqwlzy/zqwwzy/202405/t20240507_13182671.html) ("东壁居中为主尊…北壁为普贤") |
| **(revision 2026-10-05)** Cave 136 viewing: visitors do not go in; they look in from the mouth. On 22–24 September 2025 the metal barrier was replaced by a half-height wooden rail ("第136窟的铁质大门被拆除，换上了普通木质围栏"; "半人高的木质围栏"). History: steel bars from 1996, aluminium window-style railing from 2018. Both press photos show tourists outside the rail with the sutra wheel just beyond it. A 2024 expert group wrote "我们有幸进入的第136号窟", so entry is a special arrangement. Faces west: "坐东向西", 405 cm high, 679 cm deep (2009 paper), and Li Fangyin (2022). The old line "the shadows on the figures shift as you move" paraphrased "进入洞窟的时候就有步移影换之妙", which assumes walking inside. It was replaced with what a visitor at the rail sees: different figures come out from behind the central sutra case as you move along the rail. The light is now "can slant in", because no 2025–2026 visitor report confirming it was found. | [People's Daily Chongqing, 2025-09-22](http://cq.people.com.cn/n2/2025/0922/c367652-41359819.html); [CNR Chongqing, 2025-09-23](https://cq.cnr.cn/cq/20250923/t20250923_527372385.shtml); [Dazu News, 2025-09-25](https://www.txdzw.com/xw/dzxw/content_90608); [Tencent News, 2024-05-13](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20240513A00MFR00); [Fjdh: 转轮经藏窟之管见 (2009)](https://www.fjdh.cn/wumin/2009/05/06440277206.html); [West China City Daily, 2022](https://www.wccdaily.com.cn/wapepaper/html/20220427/173009.html) |

## Zhangjiajie National Forest Park (Wulingyuan)

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| UNESCO: "more than 3,000 narrow sandstone pillars and peaks, many over 200 m high"; ravines, streams, pools, waterfalls, about 40 caves and two large natural bridges; inscribed 1992. Story: "more than 3,000 sandstone pillars, many over 200 metres tall" (FAQ 1), "sandstone pillars by the hundred … many over 200 metres" (why 1). | [UNESCO 640](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/640/) (via search extract); [UNEP-WCMC World Heritage Datasheet](http://world-heritage-datasheets.unep-wcmc.org/datasheet/output/site/wulingyuan-scenic-and-historic-interest-area/) |
| Datasheet: 3,103 "wooded pillar-like peaks", more than 1,000 over 200 m, some over 400 m; "vertical joints … a major factor in the formation of these peaks"; "Fogs and patchy clouds are fairly common and are renowned for providing a dramatic and ever-varying backdrop to the peaks"; rhesus monkeys present; Wuling pine communities between 800 and 1,100 m; snow "falls some years"; rainfall peaks in early summer. | [UNEP-WCMC datasheet](http://world-heritage-datasheets.unep-wcmc.org/datasheet/output/site/wulingyuan-scenic-and-historic-interest-area/) |
| 200 m ≈ a 60-storey building ("许多岩柱都高达200米以上，相当于60层的建筑"); Tianzi Mountain is where the pillars are densest ("峰林最密集的天子山一带"); the landform goes from tableland and mesa (young) to clustered peaks and pillar forest (prime) to remnant pillars in wide valleys (old), "山体从'完整'到'破碎'". | [The Paper (澎湃号·湃客), 2021-08-31](https://www.thepaper.cn/newsDetail_forward_14262261) |
| Hunan Daily: Zhangjiajie keeps the whole sequence "平台、方山、峰墙、峰林、峰丛、残林"; "在张家界，你可以看到石英砂岩峰林的完整形成过程"; water cut down vertical joints in the flat-lying sandstone. Story: "Here you can see each stage of that in a single day" (no geology terms used). | [Voice of Hunan (Hunan Daily group), 2022-09](https://hunan.voc.com.cn/news/202209/23296682.html) |
| Official park site: the Forest Park is in the "青年期" (young stage); Huangshi Village and Yaozi Village are flat-topped mesas ("宽厚而顶平坡陡的方山"); saying "不到黄石寨，枉到张家界"; Huangshi Village top 1,080 m, "若遇云海连绵，群峰白纱披肩，峥嵘时隐时现". Tianzi Mountain is "壮年期", "素有'峰林之王'的美称". | [hnzjj.com: 黄石寨 / 张家界国家森林公园](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/3.html); [hnzjj.com: 天子山](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/1.html); Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism English page ("King of Peak Forests") |
| Yuanjiajie, average altitude 1,074 m. 迷魂台: "每当雨后初晴，时有轻云淡雾于群峰间飘浮、缭绕、但见山移峰潜…游人至此，个个神魂痴迷…故有迷魂台之誉". Story: "Mihun Tai, roughly the terrace that bewitches you". | [hnzjj.com: 袁家界](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/10.html) |
| 天下第一桥: a natural stone bridge between two peaks that "原为一体"; its weaker middle was worn away by weathering, collapse and water. Official site: height "三百多米"; UNEP-WCMC: hangs 357 m above the valley floor. **Dimensions conflict** (official 2 m wide, 20-odd m span; WCMC 40 m long, 10 m wide), so the story gives only "more than 300 metres above the valley". Rednet: "桥上苍松挺拔，桥下云雾荡漾". | [hnzjj.com: 袁家界](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/10.html); [UNEP-WCMC datasheet](http://world-heritage-datasheets.unep-wcmc.org/datasheet/output/site/wulingyuan-scenic-and-historic-interest-area/); [Rednet Zhangjiajie, 2024-03-07](https://zjj.rednet.cn/content/646847/99/13606548.html) |
| The path crosses the stone bridge; railings hung with red ribbons and locks. Weakest row: a travel article, plus the owner's own photo alt in `lib/zhangjiajieOlderTravellersI18n.ts` ("red prayer ribbons tied along its rail"). **Recheck on site if possible.** | [Sohu travel: 天下第一桥](https://www.sohu.com/a/115264845_264811); `lib/zhangjiajieOlderTravellersI18n.ts` (bridgeAlt) |
| Pines on the pillar tops: "桥上苍松挺拔" (Rednet); Korean media: "꼭대기 바위틈에 뿌리를 내린 소나무"; Hallelujah pillar "顶部植被郁郁葱葱" (official). | Rednet above; [Daum: 장가계의 진수](https://v.daum.net/v/o4Eeo67wSX?f=p); hnzjj 袁家界 |

| Bailong Elevator: running height 326 m; 154 m in a shaft inside the mountain, 172 m on a steel frame against the cliff; three double-deck glass cars; 3 m/s. 326 ÷ 3 ≈ 109 seconds, hence "in under two minutes". People's Daily: Korean groups gasp "the moment the lift leaves the shaft" (not used in text; kept as a lead). | [hnzjj.com: 袁家界](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/10.html); Forest Park guide (ticket table); [People's Daily, 2025-04-05](http://ent.people.com.cn/n1/2025/0405/c1012-40453856.html) |
| Golden Whip Stream: walking only, "一般需要2.5小时游览" / "2个半到3个小时"; "溪水明净，跌宕多姿，小鱼游弋其中。溪畔花草鲜美，鸟鸣莺啼"; "人在峡谷走，山从两边过". **Length conflicts on the same official page (7.5 km and 5.7 km)**, so the story uses time only. | [hnzjj.com: 金鞭溪](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/4.html) |
| Golden Whip Stream: towering trees, "溪水送来阵阵清凉" even in summer heat; wild monkeys "会毫不客气地来抢游客手里、包里的吃的". | [Hunan Daily / New Hunan, 2022-04-30](https://m.voc.com.cn/xhn/news/202204/14246159.html) |
| Imperial Brush Peaks (御笔峰), Tianzi Mountain: "三座石峰直指云天…无尘无土的石峰上，青松巧饰，极像一排倒插的毛笔"; the signature view of the sandstone pillars. Story: "a few slim columns with pines on top, like giant writing brushes planted handle-down". | [Wulingyuan News (district news site), 2014-05-05](https://www.wlynews.cn/content/2014/05/05/7478617.html); [The Paper, 2021](https://www.thepaper.cn/newsDetail_forward_14262261) |
| Season: Tianzi Mountain autumn colour photographed 4 November 2025 ("秋色斑斓"); sea of clouds at Tianzi Mountain on 30 March 2026, "amid the continuous spring rain"; Xinhua (2025): after rain "一柱柱峰林在云雾中若隐若现". | [Hunan Government, 2025-11-05](https://www.hunan.gov.cn/hnszf/hnyw/jdt2/202511/t20251105_33842106.html); [Hunan Government (English), 2026-04-02](https://www.enghunan.gov.cn/hneng/news_photo/202604/t20260402_33946691.html); [Xinhua, 2025-02-27](https://www.news.cn/local/20250227/777e36d448ac427db10100aafe75233e/c.html) |

## Tianmen Mountain

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| Tianmen Cave: "门高131.5米，宽57米，深60米", runs north–south through the mountain; Tianmen Mountain is 8 km from the city. | [Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism: 旅游路线](https://whhlyt.hunan.gov.cn/whhlyt/wldhlylx/202208/t20220826_27793507.html) |
| Official park pages: cave "位于海拔1260多米的绝壁之上" and elsewhere "海拔1300米", so the story says "about 1,300 metres up"; summit 1,518.6 m; "与山下市区相对高差达1300多米"; in 263 "嵩梁山忽然峭壁洞开"; the summit is "相对平坦，保存着完整的原始次森林…古树参天，藤蔓缠绕，青苔遍布". | [hnzjj.com: 天门山 (ticket page)](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Ticket/show/6.html); [hnzjj.com: 天门山](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/2.html) |
| "公元263年，嵩梁山峭壁洞开，吴王孙休以为吉祥，遂命名为'天门山'". | [New Hunan (Hunan Daily), 2017-11](https://www.hunantoday.cn/news/xhn/201711/17379074.html) |
| Ghost Valley plank road: "全长1600米，平均海拔为1400米…全线都立于万丈悬崖的中间，给人以与悬崖共起伏同屈伸的感觉". The mountain road "刚好九十九个弯". | [hnzjj.com: 天门山](http://www.hnzjj.com/index.php/Product/show/2.html) |
| Coiling Dragon Cliff glass walkway: opened 1 August 2016, 100 m long, 1.6 m wide, named for its view of the 99-bend road. | [ifeng / Visual China, 2016-08-02](https://finance.ifeng.com/a/20160802/14670046_0.shtml) |
| From 12 May 2026 the summit **east** glass walkway is closed for a full repair; Coiling Dragon Cliff and the west glass walkway stay open. | 999 steps up to the cave; escalators run beside them (the lower Dengtianmen escalators); the cable car's lower station is in the city; the upper section is suspended, so route A changes at the middle station; ice, fog or heavy rain can switch A/B to route C. People's Daily also names the "999级'天梯'". | `content/guides/tianmen-mountain-tickets-and-routes/body.en.ts`; [People's Daily, 2025-04-05](http://ent.people.com.cn/n1/2025/0405/c1012-40453856.html); [Wikipedia: Tianmen Mountain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tianmen_Mountain) |
| "Whichever way you ride up, you arrive below a stairway": the fast cableway's upper station is about a 10-minute walk from Tianmen Cave Square, where the 999 steps begin; the bus on routes A/B stops at the cave. **Weak row**: the Yongding District government page that should confirm this refused connections; the walk time comes from a travel-forum summary. | Tianmen guide (route cards); search summary of Zhihu/Ctrip route notes |
| Upper cable car closed since 6 November 2025, being dismantled and rebuilt; the lower section still runs. | `lib/zhangjiajieOlderTravellersI18n.ts` (checked with a local operator, 2026-07-23); Tianmen guide |
| Mist in the arch: "天门吐雾" is a named phenomenon (Hunan Daily, 2021); Rednet (2025): "云雾在脚下翻腾…阳光穿透云雾，洒在洞口". Story hedges: "On damp days cloud can churn inside the arch". | [New Hunan, 2021-10-03](https://www.hunantoday.cn/news/xhn/202110/17343146.html); [Rednet, 2025-07-09](https://hn.rednet.cn/content/646941/91/15112479.html) |
| Summer: "当山脚下气温高达30℃以上时，山顶的气温却维持在26℃左右"; summit forest cover 90 %. | [Rednet, 2025-07-09](https://hn.rednet.cn/content/646941/91/15112479.html) |
| Rime ice: "海拔1500余米的山巅之上，万千树木缀满晶莹冰晶", December to February, best from late December to early February. Snow doubles winter visitor numbers (2,000 → 4,000 a day), so expect crowds after snow (not stated in the story). | [Hunan Daily via Tencent News, 2025-12-04](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20251204A07AQO00); [Hunan Daily e-paper, 2026-01-09](https://hnrb.hunantoday.cn/hnrb_epaper/html/2026-01/09/content_1770197.htm) |

## Zhangjiajie Grand Canyon (glass bridge)

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| "湖南张家界大峡谷玻璃桥总长430米、宽6米，桥面距离谷底相对高度约300米，桥面全部采用透明玻璃铺设，游客走在桥上可以清楚地看到脚下悬崖峭壁，好似行在空中". **Width conflict**: English Wikipedia gives 14 m; not used. | [Xinhua, 2021-11-12](https://www.news.cn/photo/2021-11/12/c_1128059559_3.htm); [China Railway Major Bridge Engineering Group](https://www.crecg.com/web/xwzx61/zfgsdt39/2025021110083291104/index.html) |
| Architect Haim Dotan: "The Zhangjiajie Glass Bridge was designed to be as invisible as possible – a white bridge disappearing into the clouds." 430 m long, 300 m above the canyon floor. (Chinese sources credit the structural design to China Railway Major Bridge Reconnaissance & Design Institute; the story names Dotan only as the architect.) | [Dezeen, 2016-08-25](https://www.dezeen.com/2016/08/25/zhangjiajie-grand-canyon-glass-bridge-haim-dotan-walkway-china/) |
| Name 云天渡 chosen in a global naming contest (May 2016); meaning "天桥合一，以渡天下之人"; "在云雾之间，大桥宛如千尺白绫在其中若隐若现". | [Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism, 2016-05-05](https://whhlyt.hunan.gov.cn/whhlyt/news/szyw/201909/t20190912_5483682.html) |
| Official routes: A (canyon only) 3 km, about 3 hours; B (bridge + canyon) 3.5 km, about 3.5 hours: theatre → glass bridge → bungee platform → east visitor centre → Small One-Line Sky → hanging plank road → Wuwang Slope → cloud-viewing platform → treasure-hunt lift → Shenquan Stream → Rainbow Square → Tianhe Waterfall → … → Five-Colour Lake → zip line → … → Shenquan Lake. B1 (short) 1.5 km: visitor centre → One-Line Sky slide (or Rainbow Lake boat) → Rainbow Square → sculpture lift → treasure-hunt lift → bridge-east centre → glass bridge → exit at the bridge, so it goes down into the canyon first, comes back up by lift and ends on the bridge. **(revision)** The page gives no time for B1: its 预计用时 field repeats "1.5公里" (a data slip), so the story gives only the length. The glass slide is on routes A and B1, not on B, so why 3 no longer offers "a long slide" on the full route. | [Grand Canyon official site: 导览](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/guide.html) (`research/dxg-guide.html.txt`) |
| Tianhe Waterfall: "瀑布就像突然从山中绝壁喷涌而出，看不到水源…还没有走到瀑布底下就有阵阵凉风迎面扑来…只要有阳光照射，就会出现彩虹…瀑布下方的观景台比较湿滑"; the first waterfall in the canyon. | [Grand Canyon official site: 天河瀑布](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/71.html) |
| Lifts: the sightseeing lift is optional ("游客可自由选择项目"), 110 m; with the 120 m treasure-hunt lift it takes visitors from the bridge level down to the canyon floor, "缩短步行游览时间40分钟"; "全透明的电梯轿厢可以欣赏到云雾中的玻璃桥…雨后的峡谷云雾缥缈". Glass slide at One-Line Sky: 500 m long, 200 m drop. Story: "with lifts or a long slide for part of the way"; "Ride the glass lift down the canyon wall after rain and you watch the bridge come and go in the mist". | [Official site: 寻宝电梯](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/191.html); [Official site: 极限体验](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/extreme.html) |
| From below, in sunshine, the bridge "熠熠生辉，与天空融为一体" (official site's guide script). Story: "a thin white line melting into the sky". | [Official site: 关于我们](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/about.html) |
| One-Line Sky: from it to the canyon floor the path is "700多米，垂直高差为300多米" (used only as background for "goes down the canyon wall"). | [Official site: 一线天](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/98.html) |
| The walk ends with a boat on Shenquan Lake to the exit: the official A/B routes end at 神泉湖; route listings show "神泉湖游船→景区出口"; 2026 reports list a Shenquan Lake boat in route B. The Cili County page describing the boat out was unreachable. | Official guide page; [Dahepiao route list](https://www.dahepiao.com/lvyounews1/20220303258281.html) |
| Location: Sanguansi Township, Cili County, next to Wulingyuan; an official corridor bus from the city takes about 1 hour and passes Huanglong Cave on the way. "Less from Wulingyuan" follows the itinerary guide (use Wulingyuan as the base for the Canyon and Huanglong Cave). | [Hunan Road Transport Administration, 2024-05](https://jtt.hunan.gov.cn/hnygj/szdtyg/202405/t20240507_33294107.html); [Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism: 旅游路线](https://whhlyt.hunan.gov.cn/whhlyt/wldhlylx/202208/t20220826_27793507.html); `lib/zhangjiajieGuideI18n.ts` |
| Hammer and car test: on 25 June 2016, before the August opening, the deck was struck repeatedly with sledgehammers and a car was driven across it in public. | [Rednet, 2016-06-25](https://hn.rednet.cn/c/2016/06/25/1031143.htm); [Sina, 2016-06-26](http://news.sina.com.cn/o/2016-06-26/doc-ifxtmses0989138.shtml) |
| Advice not to cross: "因玻璃桥比较惊险，建议醉酒、精神异常、心脏疾病、抑郁症患者、恐高症患者、肢体残疾行动不便者不要参加游览". **This is the 2020 purchase notice**, the newest the official site shows; recheck before `ready: true`. | [Official site: 2020年最新购票须知](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/49.html) |
| No wheelchairs or walking frames on the bridge. | `lib/zhangjiajieOlderTravellersI18n.ts` (checked with a local operator, 2026-07-23) |

| Huanglong Cave: Wulingyuan District; about 7 km long; walk, then a boat on the underground river for about 15 minutes; 定海神针 about 20 m tall, 10 cm at its narrowest. Itinerary guide: Canyon + Huanglong Cave is a conditional long day (early start, compatible entry times, no evening departure); Tianmen + Canyon is not a default. | [Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism: Huanglong Cave](https://whhlyt.hunan.gov.cn/whhlyt/wldhjqjd/202208/t20220816_27583725.html); `lib/zhangjiajieGuideI18n.ts` |

| **(revision)** Glass lift (now highlight 1): "位于吴王坡山顶悬崖直达寻宝电梯中段上平台…由 3台全透明玻璃轿厢组成…游客游览完玻璃桥和玻璃栈道后，乘坐电梯可到 200多米的峡谷底部，缩短步行游览时间 40分钟…全透明的电梯轿厢可以欣赏到云雾中的玻璃桥…雨后的峡谷云雾缥缈…搭乘在其中宛如穿梭于云雾之间…该电梯属于游客可自由选择项目…拟申请核定游览观光体验价30元/人次". | **(revision)** why 1: "only clear glass lies between your shoes and the drop" (the draft's "nothing … but air" was literally false); "桥面距离谷底相对高度约300米" → "the canyon floor". why 3: "the air turns cool with spray" from the Tianhe Waterfall page ("阵阵凉风迎面扑来"); "cliffs rise on both sides" is the canyon itself. | [Xinhua, 2021-11-12](https://www.news.cn/photo/2021-11/12/c_1128059559_3.htm); [Official site: 天河瀑布](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/71.html) |
| **(revision)** FAQ 3 glass test (re-read): a randomly chosen deck panel; "整块三层夹胶桥面玻璃"; "已经裂成无数碎片的三层钢化玻璃仍然牢牢黏合成一个整体"; "这块桥面玻璃一共承受了154次铁锤重击，2次满载11人的越野车碾压，但仍保持完整". The bridge opened in August 2016. | **(revision)** `skip`: the 2020 notice advises "肢体残疾行动不便者不要参加游览" (in context: "因玻璃桥比较惊险") → "asks people with limited mobility not to go on the bridge". "Wheelchairs and walking frames are not allowed on it" is **kept** from the owner's published Older Travellers page, which states it flatly ("does not admit wheelchairs, walking frames or similar mobility aids", checked with a local operator 2026-07-23); softening it would leave two Homeground pages disagreeing. | [Official site: 购票须知](https://zjjdaxiagu.com/detail/49.html); `lib/zhangjiajieOlderTravellersI18n.ts` |
| **(revision)** Huanglong Cave needle: "最细处直径只有10厘米" → en "across at its narrowest point" (was "at its waist"). | [Hunan Dept of Culture and Tourism: Huanglong Cave](https://whhlyt.hunan.gov.cn/whhlyt/wldhjqjd/202208/t20220816_27583725.html) |

## Jade Dragon Snow Mountain (Lijiang)

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| **(rev)** Direction and view: Wikipedia puts the Old Town of Lijiang at 26.867 N, 100.233 E and the summit at 27.098 N, 100.175 E, about 26 km apart on a bearing of about 347°, so the story says "look north" (zh 往北看; ko 북쪽을), not north-west. Yunnan Daily's "位于丽江市的西北部，距离市区约15公里" places the scenic area within the municipality. Yunnan Daily also calls it "世界上少有的城市雪山" (a snow mountain seen from the city), and Wikipedia notes the view of the massif from Black Dragon Pool at the old town's edge; the view is hedged "On a clear day" because cloud hides it much of the summer. The draft's "Up close it is bigger still" was cut. | [Wikipedia: Old Town of Lijiang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Town_of_Lijiang); [Wikipedia: Jade Dragon Snow Mountain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jade_Dragon_Snow_Mountain); [Yunnan Daily via Yunnan.cn, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml) |
| The mountain is the incarnation of the Naxi protector god "三多" (Yunnan Daily's spelling); 13 peaks run north–south for nearly 35 km; 19 temperate glaciers. | [Yunnan Daily via Yunnan.cn, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml) |
| Glacier Park cable car: lower station 3,356 m, upper station 4,506 m, vertical rise 1,150 m ("more than a kilometre"); daily cap of 10,000 visitors from 22 May 2018; the Glacier Park has a loop boardwalk with added benches ("形成了环线栈道，增加了休息座椅"). 4.32 million visitors in 2018 ("millions come each year"). **(rev)** The quota is no longer offered in `skip` as evidence of crowding (a quota limits tickets, it does not show crowds); it stays in FAQ 2 as booking advice, matching the guide's "tighter quota". | [Yunnan Daily via Yunnan.cn, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml); guide `content/guides/jade-dragon-snow-mountain-cable-car-booking/` |
