| **(rev)** Vegetation on the east slope: 3,200–4,200 m "山地暗针叶林带…丽江云杉林和长苞冷杉林" (dark conifer forest of spruce and fir; story: "dark fir and spruce forest", zh 深色的冷杉、云杉林, ko 짙은 전나무·가문비나무 숲); 3,700–4,300 m alpine rhododendron scrub; 4,300–5,000 m "高山流石滩稀疏植被带" (story: "slopes of bare, broken rock", replacing the jargon "scree"; zh 光秃秃的碎石坡; ko 돌 부스러기만 깔린 비탈); the main peak carries hanging glaciers ("主峰扇子陡发育有悬冰川"). | [Chinese Wikipedia: 玉龙雪山](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%8E%89%E9%BE%99%E9%9B%AA%E5%B1%B1) |
| Lijiang city stands at about 2,400 m (the Lijiang basin averages 2,466 m). Basis for "two kilometres higher". | [Wikipedia: Lijiang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lijiang) |
| Naxi name 欧鲁, "银色的山岩" (silver rock); the mountain is the incarnation of Sanduo; the festival on the 8th day of the 2nd lunar month; "丽江白沙村北的北岳庙尚存，并是举行三朵节的重要场地". | [Chinese Wikipedia: 玉龙雪山](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%8E%89%E9%BE%99%E9%9B%AA%E5%B1%B1) |
| **(rev)** Sanduo legend, re-read in full: Sanduo "骑白马、穿白甲、戴白盔、执白矛"; the hunter found a snow-stone "很大，像魁梧的武将，但又非常轻，一只手便能把它托起" (story adds "as big as a burly warrior"); "走到山麓，猎人放下雪石，抽袋烟又背，可是哪里背得动…纹丝不动…便就地建祠供奉" (story now includes the rest stop in English: "after resting at the foot of the mountain he could not lift it again"); "三朵神则源于白沙玉龙村的北岳庙会". So the temple is "near Baisha" (zh 白沙一带; ko 바이샤 근처), not "in Baisha village". The story does not claim the temple stands on the legend's spot. | [China Ethnic Culture Resource Database: 纳西族三朵节](http://www.minwang.com.cn/mzwhzyk/663688/686141/686146/636024/index.html) (http only; `research/minwang_sanduo.html`) |
| **(rev)** Lunar date in English: the 8th day of the 2nd lunar month fell on 27 Feb 2023, 17 Mar 2024, 7 Mar 2025 and 26 Mar 2026 (read from the Hong Kong Observatory's Gregorian–lunar tables), so "a lunar date in late February or March". ko adds "(양력 2월 말~3월)"; zh keeps 农历二月初八. | [Hong Kong Observatory: Gregorian–Lunar Calendar Conversion Table 2026](https://www.hko.gov.hk/tc/gts/time/calendar/text/files/T2026c.txt) (and T2023c–T2025c; `research/hko_T*.txt`) |
| **(rev)** Baishui No. 1 glacier: "玉龙雪山最大的白水1号冰川"; "海拔4680米处的冰川宽度由2004年的336米缩减为2009年的318米" (the glacier reaches the lookout's height); its tongue retreated about 250 m from 1982 to 2002; global warming is the main cause. Glacier Park is where visitors see Baishui No. 1 (Baidu Baike: "夏、秋季可观赏风景区域长2.7公里…白水一号冰川"; Chinese Wikipedia: "白水一号是目前最具游览条件的冰川"). A 2024 peer-reviewed survey: the glacier "has experienced significant ablation due to regional warming during the past few decades", and its terminus retreated 16.35 m a year on average between 2018 and 2021. Story: "The glacier in front of you, Baishui No. 1, is the largest on the mountain, and it has been shrinking for decades as the climate warms." The 1982–2002 figure and the word "tongue" were cut. | [National Climate Center (CMA), 2009](https://www.ncc-cma.net/channel/news/newsid/4230) (`research/cma4230.html`); [Baidu Baike: 冰川公园](https://baike.baidu.com/item/%E5%86%B0%E5%B7%9D%E5%85%AC%E5%9B%AD/2236188); [Chinese Wikipedia: 玉龙雪山](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%8E%89%E9%BE%99%E9%9B%AA%E5%B1%B1); [Atmosphere 15(2):231, 2024, doi:10.3390/atmos15020231](https://doi.org/10.3390/atmos15020231) (abstract via Crossref, `research/crossref_atmos.json`) |
| **(rev)** Spruce Meadow: a forest clearing of about 0.5 km² at 3,240 m on the east side; Naxi legend makes it the way to the "玉龙第三国"; Dongba scripture: "火红斑虎当乘骑，银角花鹿来耕耘……花尾锦鸡来报晓". Its other name, 情死之地, refers to Naxi lovers who died there hoping to reach that paradise; the story says only "a paradise sought by lovers who were not allowed to marry". The cable car: Baidu Baike (via search snippet; the page returns 403 here) gives 8-seat enclosed cabins, about 1,000 m, about 3 minutes, ending at the meadow ringed by spruce, with boardwalks; a travel agency page gives "只需10分钟". Sources disagree on the time, so the story gives none and calls it "the smaller cable car" (the Chinese names are 大索道 and 小索道). | [Chinese Wikipedia: 玉龙雪山 (云杉坪)](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%8E%89%E9%BE%99%E9%9B%AA%E5%B1%B1); [Baidu Baike: 云杉坪索道](https://baike.baidu.com/item/%E4%BA%91%E6%9D%89%E5%9D%AA%E7%B4%A2%E9%81%93/17397509); [Chongqing Meiya Travel: 玉龙雪山有3条索道选哪个](https://m.57023.com/yulongxueshan/a334.html) |
| Blue Moon Valley was formerly the White Water River (白水河): its bed and terraces are white marble and limestone fragments, fed by glacier and snow meltwater; the crescent-shaped valley's water looks blue in sunshine and white when it rains. | [Chinese National Geography (dili360): 蓝月谷](http://tg.dili360.com/scenic/place/id/3625.htm) (http only); [Chinese Wikipedia: 玉龙雪山 (白水河, 蓝月谷)](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%8E%89%E9%BE%99%E9%9B%AA%E5%B1%B1) |
| **(rev)** Blue Moon Valley: six sediment-check dams formed nearly 300,000 m³ of water in four lakes, now an important water source for Lijiang city and the old town's landscape water. The year it was built (2006) was cut from the story. | [Yunnan Daily via Yunnan.cn, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml) |
| Glacier Park and Spruce Meadow are separate cable-car products on top of entry; Glacier Park has the tighter quota and is weather-sensitive; wind or weather can suspend a cable car; Yak Meadow has been closed for rebuilding since 4 March 2026 (not mentioned). | `content/guides/jade-dragon-snow-mountain-cable-car-booking/` (body.en.ts, source-log.md); `lib/attractionReservations.ts` (`jade-dragon-snow-mountain`) |
| **(rev)** Official health advice: "部分游客可能因为高海拔而产生高原反应，如有不适，请及时休息或就医，有心脏病、高血压、孕妇等等有病史和不适宜人群不能乘坐冰川公园索道" (story FAQ 5 now says "rest at once or get medical help"; zh 及时休息或就医; ko 바로 쉬거나 진료를 받으세요). The same page points those people to Yak Meadow (closed for rebuilding since 4 March 2026, per the guide) or Blue Moon Valley, so the story names Blue Moon Valley as the official alternative and says only that Spruce Meadow (3,240 m) is "far lower than Glacier Park, but still above 3,000 metres"; it no longer implies Spruce Meadow is officially suitable for these visitors; bring down jackets, scarves and gloves; bus 101 from the old town via Black Dragon Pool to the mountain takes about 50 minutes ("under an hour by road"). | [Lijiang Municipal Media Centre, 2024](https://www.lijiang.cn/article/127342.html) (`research/lj127342.html`) |
| On 19 March 2025 sudden heavy snow and icy roads closed the Yak Meadow, Spruce Meadow and Glacier Park cable cars. **(rev)** The dated sentence was cut from `when`; the source stays in `meta` because it backs "wind or snow can stop the cable cars at short notice". | [Guangming Daily, 2025](https://m.gmw.cn/2025-03/19/content_1303995430.htm) |
| **(rev)** Lijiang climate (city station, 2,381 m): winters very dry and sunny; about 80 % of the 968 mm annual rain falls June to September; percent possible sunshine from 29 % in July to 80 % in December. The story now says "December is Lijiang's sunniest month" and "in July the peaks often hide in cloud", and no longer presents the city's sunshine percentages as summit conditions. | [Wikipedia: Lijiang (climate)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lijiang#Climate) |
| "一山分四季，十里不同天" is how the scenic area describes the cable-car ride (zh `when`). | [Yunnan Daily via Yunnan.cn, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml) |
| Black Dragon Pool: north edge of the old town; views of the mountain over its white marble bridge; the reflection with the bridge and 得月楼 is Lijiang's classic postcard; the level varies, hence "when the pool is full". It is on the bus 101 route from the old town to the mountain ("on the road out to the mountain"). | [Wikipedia: Black Dragon Pool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_Dragon_Pool); [Chinese National Geography (dili360): 黑龙潭](http://tg.dili360.com/scenic/place/id/1319.htm) (http only); [Lijiang Municipal Media Centre, 2024](https://www.lijiang.cn/article/127342.html) |
| **(rev)** Tiger Leaping Gorge: the massif's north-western flank forms one side of the gorge, with the trekking route on the other side; the gorge is 60 km north of Lijiang; the high road is "approximately 22 kilometres" with "a fair number of guesthouses for trekkers" and is inadvisable in the rainy season. Story: "if you have strong legs and a day or two, walk the high trail" (zh 腿脚好、又有一两天时间的话; ko 다리 힘과 하루이틀 여유가 있다면). | [Wikipedia: Jade Dragon Snow Mountain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jade_Dragon_Snow_Mountain); [Wikipedia: Tiger Leaping Gorge](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tiger_Leaping_Gorge) |
| Korean names: 옥룡설산, 리장 (sightsI18n); 빙천공원, 윈산핑 (guide body.ko.ts and the reservation rule; 운삼평 glossed once); 남월곡 (tour product); 흑룡담 (visit-yunnan KR, Trip.com KR); 호도협; 나시족, 동파경(東巴經), 백수 1호 빙하, 백수하(白水河), 바이샤(白沙). **(rev)** 삼다(三朵) → 삼다(三多): 삼다 is the reading of 三多 (朵 reads 타); Yunnan Daily writes 三多, Chinese sources use both. zh keeps 三朵 (三朵节 is the festival's usual written form). | `lib/sightsI18n.ts`; guide body.ko.ts; `lib/privateTourExpansionProducts.ts`; [Trip.com KR: 옥룡설산](https://kr.trip.com/guide/attraction/%EC%98%A5%EB%A3%A1%EC%84%A4%EC%82%B0.html); [visit-yunnan KR: 흑룡담](https://visit-yunnan.com/ko/blog/black-dragon-pool-lijiang); [Yunnan Daily, 2019](https://yn.yunnan.cn/system/2019/06/26/030308776.shtml) |
| Wikidata: Q1465660 (Jade Dragon Snow Mountain). Not a UNESCO site (Lijiang's Old Town, 811, is separate), so sameAs has no UNESCO URL. | [Wikidata Q1465660](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q1465660) |

**(rev)** `meta.sources` order: Lijiang Municipal Media Centre (city government outlet) first, then Yunnan Daily, CMA and Guangming Daily.

Open points:
- Summit height: Wikipedia and most sources give 5,596 m; a newer survey figure of 5,590.2 m also circulates. The story gives no summit height.
- Impression Lijiang (Zhang Yimou show at Ganhaizi) was left out: several 2026 posts say it has been long suspended, but no official notice was found either way.
- "Closest glacier to the equator in Eurasia" and "Asia's highest sightseeing cable car" are not used.
- The candidate official site yulongxueshan.cn served a certificate for another host and a 404 over http, so it is not cited.
- Spruce Meadow cable-car ride time: about 3 minutes (Baidu Baike snippet) or about 10 minutes (travel agency); no figure is used.

## Chen Clan Ancestral Hall

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| Built by Chen clans of 72 Guangdong counties as a joint clan hall ("合族祠"); planning began 1888 (光绪十四年). Completion is given as 1893 (光绪十九年: China Daily, Yangcheng Evening News via the provincial Overseas Chinese Affairs Office, Bendibao) and 1894 (Liwan District, wglj, Wikipedia), so the story says "the 1890s". | [Liwan District government](https://www.lw.gov.cn/zjlw/lwdt/yzlw/content/post_9051669.html); [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_9773088.html); [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Guangdong Overseas Chinese Affairs Office (Yangcheng Evening News)](http://www.qb.gd.gov.cn/jrqx/content/post_1246013.html) |
| Purpose: temporary lodging for clan sons coming to the provincial capital "备考科举、候任、交纳赋税、办理诉讼"; a senior museum guide (曾翔) likens such halls to each county's "驻广州办事处". | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Guangdong Overseas Chinese Affairs Office](http://www.qb.gd.gov.cn/jrqx/content/post_1246013.html) |
| The board over the gate reads 陈氏书院. After the exams were abolished in 1905 it became 陈氏实业学堂, then schools; 1959 Guangdong Folk Art Gallery, renamed Guangdong Folk Art Museum in 1994. | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_9773088.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall); [Guangdong Overseas Chinese Affairs Office](http://www.qb.gd.gov.cn/jrqx/content/post_1246013.html) |
| Decoration in seven crafts ("三雕、二塑、一铸、一画": wood, stone and brick carving; pottery and plaster figures; iron casting; painting). Basis for "carved, moulded or cast". Ridge themes are mainly Cantonese opera stories, flowers and lucky motifs; the ridges are brightly coloured ("色彩斑斓"). | [China News Service (People's Daily Overseas), 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml); [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html) |
| Two painted door gods about 4 m tall on the tall black-lacquered doors, "广东地区现存最大的彩绘门神". **(revision)** The record is no longer used: Chinese Wikipedia says both door gods were gouged with chisels in 1966 and repainted to the original design after the 1981 restoration, so "surviving" was wrong, and the record does nothing on site. The story keeps only the 4 m door gods on the black doors. | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2026](http://gz.bendibao.com/news/2026730/content370420.shtml) |
| The screen behind the gate: a big banana tree (家大业大) and a hen leading seven chicks (儿孙昌盛); bamboo twisted into 福. | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html) |
| The main ridge on 聚贤堂: 27 m long, 2.9 m high (4.26 m with its plaster base), 224 figures, themes such as 群仙祝寿 and 加官进爵, described as "一个巨大的立体连环画"; at its ends a pair of 鳌鱼 with tails raised high and two long whiskers reaching to the sky ("独占鳌头"). 独占鳌头 = coming first in the imperial exams. **(revision)** The story now says "more than 200" figures (the owner's rule on counts). The 鳌鱼 on this ridge are now sourced to Guangzhou Daily 2006 (see the revision table), not to this row's sources. | [China News Service, 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| The ridge came from Shiwan (石湾陶, made in Shiwan, Foshan); the main ridge is attributed to the 文如璧 workshop. | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); search summary of Chinese sources (attribution not used) |
| Six large framed brick panels on the front walls either side of the gate, including 《梁山聚义》 and 《刘庆伏狼驹》 (folk tale: Western Xia sends the Song court a wild horse, the "wolf colt"; Liu Qing, under Di Qing, tames it; 40+ figures); lines "纤细如丝" (挂线砖雕). | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_9773088.html); [China News Service, 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml); [Tencent News, 2020](https://view.inews.qq.com/a/20200912A0J39400) (search summary) |
| The side lanes (青云巷) have fine line-carved grey brick ("线条如发丝般精细"); halls are joined by covered corridors (风雨连廊). | [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2026](http://gz.bendibao.com/news/2026730/content370420.shtml); [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| 聚贤堂 is set out as it was for clan meetings; its double-sided openwork wooden screens carry different stories on each face (count given as 16 by Bendibao and 12 by Wikipedia, so no number is used). The corridors and terrace use cast iron, "西方园林常见的铁铸工艺，配以中式传统雕花图案". **(revision)** "A different story on each face" is dropped: only the Bendibao listicle says it, and English Wikipedia says only that each screen shows a classic scene. The story now says the screens are carved on both faces and right through (双面镂雕), with scenes from old tales such as the Three Kingdoms. | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2026](http://gz.bendibao.com/news/2026730/content370420.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| The museum holds 20,000+ objects (ceramics, Canton embroidery, carving); craftspeople demonstrate regularly ("定期") in a demonstration hall: olive-stone carving, Canton porcelain painting, gold beating, Shiwan pottery and others. Ivory carving is also demonstrated but is left out of the story. | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Guangdong Overseas Chinese Affairs Office](http://www.qb.gd.gov.cn/jrqx/content/post_1246013.html) |
| Practical (not restated in detail): booking through the museum's official WeChat account; from 1 January 2025 domestic and foreign visitors scan valid ID at the gate; Bendibao suggests 2–3 hours; Metro Lines 1 and 8, Chen Clan Academy station. At Spring Festival 2026 the museum capped numbers and released extra same-day tickets each afternoon. Hours, prices and closing day are left out. | [Guangdong Folk Arts Museum official site](https://www.gzcjc.com.cn/); [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2026](http://gz.bendibao.com/news/2026730/content370420.shtml); [Tencent News, 2026-02-12](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20260212A0526300) |
| Foshan Ancestral Temple: Shiwan ceramic figure ridge 31.7 m long ("花脊之王"), longer than Chen Clan's 27 m. Basis for the `skip` line. | [China Cultural Relics Net (Foshan Daily), on the temple's restoration](http://www.wenwuchina.com/a/169/71906.html) |
| Metro Line 1 runs Huangsha – Changshou Lu – Chen Clan Academy (two stops); Huangsha is a Line 1/Line 6 interchange next to Shamian. | [Wikipedia: Huangsha station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huangsha_station); [Wikipedia: Chen Clan Academy station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Academy_station) |
| Xiguan: the old area west of the walled city, now most of Liwan District. Yongqingfang is on Enning Road. | [Wikipedia: Xiguan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xiguan); [Wikidata Q63925707](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q63925707) |
| Guangzhou climate: monsoon season April–September; October–December "the best travel time". | [Wikipedia: Guangzhou](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guangzhou) |
| Korean: 진가사 (as `lib/sightsI18n.ts`; also used by Korean travel blogs such as Tripstore with 陳家祠); 진씨서원 for 陈氏书院; 융칭팡 (Trip.com KR "시관 융칭팡"); 월극 for Cantonese opera; 장원 급제 for 独占鳌头. | [Tripstore KR](https://www.tripstore.kr/blog/%EA%B4%91%EC%A0%80%EC%9A%B0-%EC%97%AC%ED%96%89-%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C-3%EB%B0%95-4%EC%9D%BC-%EC%97%AD%EC%82%AC-%ED%98%84%EB%8C%80-%ED%95%B5%EC%8B%AC-%EB%AA%85%EC%86%8C); [Trip.com KR](https://kr.trip.com/moments/theme/poi-xiguan-yongqingfang-tourist-area-58280089-flower-viewing-990796/) |
| Identifiers: English Wikipedia "Chen Clan Ancestral Hall"; Wikidata Q5090739 (label "Chen Clan Academy"; aliases 陈家祠堂, 陈氏书院, 广东民间工艺博物馆). Not a UNESCO site. | [Wikidata](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q5090739) |

## Canton Tower

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| Total height 600 m: main body 454 m plus a 146 m antenna mast (official city figures). Wikipedia gives 450 + 150 = 604 m; the story uses 600 m. Opened to visitors in 2010, for the Asian Games. | [Guangzhou International (eguangzhou.gov.cn)](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/cantontower/content/post_31829.html); [Haizhu District government](https://www.haizhu.gov.cn/hzdt/ztlm/tzhz/rjhj/lyjd/csmp/content/post_9190964.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| The tower stands where the city's new axis meets the Pearl River, facing Haixinsha, Huacheng Square and the Zhujiang New Town business district across the water. | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_8928935.html) |
| Shape: a concrete core inside a steel outer frame formed by two ellipses, larger below and smaller above, offset and rotated 135° against each other, giving the slender waist; 24 steel columns and 46 rings; "镂空、开放的结构形式… 从任意角度看都不会出现重复的形态". Wikipedia: the lattice is porous at the bottom, tight at the waist "like a twisted rope", and opens again above. | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_8928935.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| Lighting: the tower glows from light fittings on the structure itself, each node controllable for animation and colour change across the whole height; colours shift through orange, red, purple and blue ("塔上持续不断变幻出橙红紫蓝等色彩"). | [Wikipedia (architectural lighting design)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower); [China News Service (Lianhe Zaobao), 2014](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/hb/2014/07-31/6446391.shtml) |
| Light schedule: sources disagree (Bendibao 2023: on at 19:00, off at 23:00; a 2025 guide: earlier in winter, later in summer). The story says only that the lights come on in the evening, stay on until late, and shift with season and holidays. | [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2023](http://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023427/ly340248.shtml); [Sohu, 2025](https://www.sohu.com/a/858287510_121924581) |
| Nickname 小蛮腰 from Bai Juyi's line "樱桃樊素口，杨柳小蛮腰" (his household singer Fansu and dancer Xiaoman). | [China News Service, 2014](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/hb/2014/07-31/6446391.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| Indoor viewing halls at 428 m and 433 m; open-air deck at the 450 m roof (Wikipedia: "just above 452 metres"); the 488 m deck on the mast is the highest point visitors can reach. | [Guangzhou Bendibao](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023112/ly332933.shtml); [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_8928935.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| Bubble cabins: 16 transparent "crystal" cabins, 3.2 m across, on a track tilted about 15° round the roof edge, kept level by a levelling device; one circuit 20 min (city summary) or 20–40 min (Wikipedia), so the story says "twenty minutes or more". Bendibao calls it the tower's most popular ride. **(revision)** "Most popular ride" is dropped (a listings-site superlative). | [Guangzhou Bendibao](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023112/ly332933.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| Sky Drop (极速云霄): on the mast between 455 m and 485 m; seated or standing drop; 30 m in just over a second, up to 80 km/h. Its Guinness title is not used. **(revision)** "In just over a second" is dropped: a 30 m fall takes about 2.5 s from rest, and at least 1.35 s even at 80 km/h. The story says only that it plunges you 30 m. | [Guangzhou Bendibao](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023112/ly332933.shtml); [Haizhu District government](https://www.haizhu.gov.cn/hzdt/ztlm/tzhz/rjhj/lyjd/csmp/content/post_9190964.html) |
| Tickets come in tiers: a basic 433 m sightseeing ticket, with the bubble cabins and other options in dearer packages. Real-name purchase and entry. The story says only that the rides and the top deck cost more and that foreign visitors use their passport. | [Guangzhou Bendibao: ticket list](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/201572/ly191881.shtml); [Guangzhou Bendibao](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023112/ly332933.shtml) |
| The Spider Walk / Sky Ladder (open-air stairs, 168–334 m, 1,088 steps) exists, but its current status could not be confirmed, so it is left out. | [Guangzhou Bendibao](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2023112/ly332933.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| Haixin Bridge: the first pedestrian bridge across the Pearl River, just west of the tower, 488 m long, curving, linking the tower side with Er'sha Island and Zhujiang New Town. Since 28 January 2025 there is no booking and it is open into the late evening, with a bag check. The story says "about 500 metres" and "curving footbridge", with no hours. | [Guangzhou City Construction Investment Group](https://www.gzci.net/groupbusiness/info.aspx?itemid=282&lcid=7); [Guangzhou Daily, 2025](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202501/26/139995_54780936.htm); [eguangzhou.gov.cn](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/cantontower/content/post_31829.html) |
| Huacheng Square holds the Guangdong Museum and the Guangzhou Opera House; the museum needs booking through its WeChat account. The museum is about 1 km from the tower (Wikipedia coordinates). **(revision)** The 1 km is a straight line across the river. On foot over the Haixin Bridge it is about 2.5 km, so the story now says "about half an hour's walk" (see the revision table). | [Guangzhou Planning and Natural Resources Bureau: new-axis city walk](https://ghzyj.gz.gov.cn/zwgk/ztzl/qgchfxcr/d21gqgchfxcr/xccl/content/post_9834651.html) |
| Pearl River night cruises: routes take 50–90 minutes; piers include Dashatou, Tianzi and the Canton Tower pier. | [Guangzhou Bendibao: night-cruise piers](https://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2024716/ly351456.shtml); [Guangzhou Daily, 2026-09-30](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202609/30/139995_55009028.htm) |
| Season: October has the most sunshine (51 % of possible) and March the least (17 %); October–December is the best travel time. | [Wikipedia: Guangzhou (climate)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guangzhou) |
| Korean: 광저우 타워 (Korean Wikipedia, `lib/sightsI18n.ts`); 화청광장 for Huacheng Square (JoongAng Economy News; one other source uses 화성광장); 주장 신도시 (Trip.com KR); 하이신교 and 하이신사 are transliterations with Chinese glosses, not checked against a Korean booking site. Lotte World Tower 555 m is a Korean hook (488 m is lower). **(revision)** The Lotte World Tower comparison is removed (ko only, and deck against building height). | [Korean Wikipedia: 광저우 타워](https://ko.wikipedia.org/wiki/%EA%B4%91%EC%A0%80%EC%9A%B0_%ED%83%80%EC%9B%8C); [JoongAng Economy News](https://www.joongangenews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=469931); [Trip.com KR](https://kr.trip.com/moments/theme/destination-zhujiang-new-town-guangdong-museum-2036576-guides-993135/) |
| Identifiers: English Wikipedia "Canton Tower"; Wikidata Q168400 (ko label 광저우 타워). | [Wikidata](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q168400) |

## Shamian Island

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| Area 0.30 km²; about 900 m east–west and 300 m north–south; bounded by the Pearl River (Bai'etan, White Goose Pool) to the south and the Shaji canal (沙基涌) to the north. | [Guangzhou International (eguangzhou.gov.cn)](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/shamian/content/post_31831.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| 137 registered old and notable trees: 68 camphors, the rest mostly banyans; two over 300 years old, both camphors; 40 over 200 and 95 over 100. | [Guangzhou Daily, 2022](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202205/30/139995_54276613.htm); [eguangzhou.gov.cn](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/shamian/content/post_31831.html) |
| The scent: "去过沙面的街坊，不少人都会发现，这里的空气弥漫着一股幽幽的香味… 这是岛上古樟树"; the smell grows stronger when the trees are pruned ("每到修枝的时候，香樟的味道会变浓"). **(revision)** The pruning line is dropped: it is one former resident's childhood memory, not something a visitor can count on. The highlight now uses crushing a fallen leaf (see the revision table). | [Guangzhou Daily, 2022](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202205/30/139995_54276613.htm) |
| One of the two 300+ camphors stands beside the Victory Hotel (胜利宾馆) on Shamian Fourth Street, 338 years old in 2022; the hotel began as the Shamian Hotel, built 1888, renamed Victoria Hotel in 1895. | [Guangzhou Daily, 2022](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202205/30/139995_54276613.htm) |
| Shamian Street: car-free; students on wooden benches, artists painting the buildings, couples taking wedding photos; bronze statues of everyday old Guangzhou (《童年》《卖麦芽糖》《读书》《寄信》); 200-year-old camphors and 100-year-old banyans. A garden walkway with flower beds runs down the middle of the street. Children sketching and people posing in front of the old buildings (Xinhua photos, April 2024). **(revision)** "Car-free" is softened to "almost empty of cars" / 几乎不见车 / 차가 거의 다니지 않는: the 2019 renewal plan only tightened outside traffic, and parking is kept for the hotels and some offices. | [People's Daily Overseas Edition, 2023](https://paper.people.com.cn/rmrbhwb/html/2023-02/09/content_25964036.htm); [Xinhua, 2024](https://www.news.cn/politics/20240424/f4ace8da3c1a451b8c95a8c32981e3c3/c.html); [New Express](https://epaper.xkb.com.cn/view/1149588) (search summary) |
| Architecture: neo-Baroque, Gothic, veranda-style, neoclassical and mixed buildings; Western-plan detached houses with hipped roofs and large verandas. | [Xinhua, 2024](https://www.news.cn/politics/20240424/f4ace8da3c1a451b8c95a8c32981e3c3/c.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| 1859: the island became the British and French concessions; China dug a canal 30 m wide and 1,200 m long along the north side (1859 to summer 1861) and built an east and a west bridge. Iron gates at the bridges, the West Bridge guarded by British police; bridges closed at night (Wikipedia: at 10 pm). The British/French split is given as 4/5–1/5 or 3/5–2/5, so it is not used. | [Yangcheng Evening News, 2021](https://ysln.ycwb.com/content/2021-12/23/content_40469952.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| West Bridge (英格兰桥): built 1861, 31.55 m long, three brick arches on granite piers; restored and in use. | [Yangcheng Evening News, 2021](https://ysln.ycwb.com/content/2021-12/23/content_40469952.html) |
| More than ten countries had consulates on Shamian, with 9 foreign banks and 40+ foreign firms; the story says "consulates from more than ten countries" and "foreign banks and trading houses". | [eguangzhou.gov.cn](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/shamian/content/post_31831.html) |
| Named buildings: the former French post office (法国邮政局旧址) and the former 万国宝通银行 (the old Chinese name of Citibank, an American bank) on Shamian Street. | [Xinhua, 2024](https://www.news.cn/politics/20240424/f4ace8da3c1a451b8c95a8c32981e3c3/c.html); [Chinese Wikipedia: 花旗银行](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E8%8A%B1%E6%97%97%E9%93%B6%E8%A1%8C) |
| Our Lady of Lourdes Chapel, 14 Shamian Street, the French Catholic chapel: Wikipedia says completed 1892; some Chinese listings give 1890 (光绪十六年), so the story says "finished in 1892". It is still a Catholic church with daily morning services. **(revision)** The year is dropped (sources disagree, and it does not help on site). The story says the French built it at their east end of the island (English Wikipedia: "Located at the French end of the island"; the French concession and bridge were at the east end). | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian); 360 Map / Ctrip listings (search summary) |
| Many buildings now house hotels, restaurants, cafés, shops and companies. | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian); [eguangzhou.gov.cn](https://www.eguangzhou.gov.cn/gzspecialreports/intlblocks/details/shamian/content/post_31831.html) |
| White Swan Hotel: opened 6 February 1983, "内地首家五星级酒店"; Henry Fok insisted on "四门大开" when even unstarred guesthouses checked papers and registered visitors at the door; 400 rolls of toilet paper were used on the first day (not used in the story). Today the first two floors are open to the public free; the three-storey atrium garden with a waterfall, "故乡水", with Henry Fok's line "别来此处最牵萦", is where Guangzhou families pose for photos. (Other sources say "first Sino-foreign joint-venture five-star hotel"; the Southern Daily wording is used.) **(revision)** "First five-star" is dropped in all three languages. China's star ratings came later: the White Swan was rated five-star in February 1990, as one of the first group (English and Chinese Wikipedia). The story now says it opened in 1983, in the first years of opening up, and keeps the open-door story from Southern Daily ("不要说五星级宾馆，就是不上星级的招待所，来访都要在大门口看证件、登记"). | [Southern Daily](https://news.southcn.com/node_8e2690394f/a15b8ea403.shtml); [Jiemian, 2019](https://www.jiemian.com/article/3010882.html) |
| Greater Bay Area Art Centre at Bai'etan, opened 2024, faces Shamian and the White Swan across the river ("北望沙面岛、广州白天鹅宾馆") and is lit at night in the city's riverside lighting. | [Guangzhou government, 2024](https://www.gz.gov.cn/zlgz/tsgz/content/post_9638937.html) (search summary); [Guangzhou Daily, 2026-09-30](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202609/30/139995_55009028.htm) |
| Night cruises slow down at Bai'etan, from where the Shamian buildings are in view. | [Guangzhou Daily, 2026-09-30](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202609/30/139995_55009028.htm) |
| Shangxiajiu: pedestrian shopping street in Liwan, about 1,200 m long with 300+ shops; about 1 km north of Shamian (Wikidata coordinates). | [Wikipedia: Shangxiajiu Pedestrian Street](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shangxiajiu_Pedestrian_Street) |
| Huangsha station (Lines 1 and 6) is next to Shamian Island. | [Wikipedia: Huangsha station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huangsha_station) |
| Season: hot and wet April–September; October–December most comfortable. | [Wikipedia: Guangzhou (climate)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guangzhou) |
| Korean: 사면도 (as `lib/sightsI18n.ts`; Tripstore KR "사면도 (샤면도/沙面島)"; one Trip.com KR post uses 샤미엔다오); 녹나무 for camphor, 반얀나무 for banyan; 화이트스완 호텔, 승리호텔, 루르드 성모 성당, 바이어탄 대만구 예술센터 and 상하구 보행거리 (Daum cafe "상하구(上下九)보행가") are given with Chinese glosses. | [Tripstore KR](https://www.tripstore.kr/blog/%EA%B4%91%EC%A0%80%EC%9A%B0-%EC%97%AC%ED%96%89-%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C-3%EB%B0%95-4%EC%9D%BC-%EC%97%AD%EC%82%AC-%ED%98%84%EB%8C%80-%ED%95%B5%EC%8B%AC-%EB%AA%85%EC%86%8C) |
| Identifiers: English Wikipedia "Shamian" (Shamian_Island redirects); Wikidata Q529977 (aliases Shameen, 沙面, 拾翠洲). Not a UNESCO site. | [Wikidata](https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Q529977) |

## Revision after review round 1 (2026-10-04)

Facts added or changed in `final.ts`, each re-checked in this revision. The draft log is backed up as `.sources.draft-backup.md`; fetched pages are in `rv1/` and `review1/`.

### Chen Clan Ancestral Hall

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| **(revision)** Pottery ridges are on some roofs, not all: China News counts 11 Shiwan pottery ridges ("陈家祠共有１１条这样的陶塑花脊"; English Wikipedia: "11 pottery ridge crest… on the nine great halls"); the rest of the ridge work is plaster. The story now says "the ridge above the gate" in `why[0]`, and "roof ridges"/"the roofs" elsewhere, never "every". | [China News Service, 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| **(revision)** The gate ridge: the middle of the south face of the front hall's ridge is a large group of opera figures among pavilions and towers, with four little balconies, each with a figure leaning on the rail to watch the street opera ("二楼有四个独立小阳台，各探出一个人头，身体倚靠护栏，聚精会神地观看街面唱戏"). Basis for "a whole opera cast among little pavilions" and the four spectators. The floor count differs between copies (two or three storeys), so no storey is named. | [Guangzhou Daily via Sina, 2017-12-29: 陈家祠屋脊上的风景](https://news.sina.com.cn/s/2017-12-29/doc-ifypyuve2128722.shtml) |
| **(revision)** Door gods: "两扇高大的黑漆木门上，绘制着两个4米高的门神" (one on each door). Gouged in 1966, repainted after 1981 (so no "surviving" record). | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Chinese Wikipedia: 陈家祠](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E9%99%88%E5%AE%B6%E7%A5%A0) |
| **(revision)** The banana plant and the hen with her chicks are on the carved screen directly inside the gate ("跨过门槛，迎面隔挡外部视线的屏门上… 大芭蕉象征家大业大，芭蕉树下一只母鸡带着7只小鸡觅食，象征儿孙昌盛"; China News: "屏门当中有块…芭蕉树和母鸡带着数只小鸡"). | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [China News Service, 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml) |
| **(revision)** The 鳌鱼 (fish-dragon) on the 聚贤堂 ridge: "鳌鱼的两根长须伸向天空… 也隐藏状元及第，独占鳌头之意"; English Wikipedia: the tips of the ridge crests are "flying cods… half-dragon half-fish", with "tentacles reaching out from the roof to the sky". | [Guangzhou Daily via Sina, 2006-09-21: 陈家祠处处巧寓“如意吉祥”](https://news.sina.cn/sa/2006-09-21/detail-ikknscsk0973170.d.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| **(revision)** The 聚贤堂 screens: double-sided openwork screens with history and story scenes ("十六扇以历史故事为题材的双面镂雕屏门"); English Wikipedia: "double-sided wood carving screen doors in the back of the Gathering Hall, each describing a classic drama scene in old story books like the Romance of the Three Kingdoms and The biography of Yue Fei". Openwork carving lets light and air through (China News, of the gate screens: "镂空而成… 能通风采光"). | [China News Service, 2011](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/cul/2011/04-19/2983043.shtml); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chen_Clan_Ancestral_Hall) |
| **(revision)** The railings in front of 聚贤堂 are stone, set with cast-iron openwork panels ("中路一、二进间的院子内有石栏杆镶嵌的铁铸双通花栏板"); the iron craft is "西方园林常见的铁铸工艺，配以中式传统雕花图案". | [Chinese Wikipedia: 陈家祠](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E9%99%88%E5%AE%B6%E7%A5%A0); [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html) |
| **(revision)** Demonstrations: "博物馆还开辟民间工艺展演厅，工艺师驻场表演…榄雕、广彩瓷…"; still current in 2025 (reviewer's check of Yangcheng Evening News, search summary). | [China Daily, 2020](https://cn.chinadaily.com.cn/a/202004/21/WS5e9e643aa310c00b73c78783.html); [Yangcheng Evening News, 2025](https://ysln.ycwb.com/content/2025-03/06/content_53275377.html) |
| **(revision)** "On big public holidays numbers can be capped" is now a hedge (the only source is the 2026 Spring Festival arrangement). | [Tencent News, 2026-02-12](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20260212A0526300) (draft cache; now 403) |
| **(revision)** FAQ 5 no longer gives 1905 or 1959; it says only that the hall became a school after the exams were abolished and now houses the Guangdong Folk Art Museum. | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_9773088.html) |
| **(revision)** Walking: Chen Clan (23.1298, 113.2401) to Yongqingfang (23.1175, 113.2329) is 1.56 km straight at a bearing of about 208°, between south and south-west, so "about 25 minutes' walk to the south-west". FAQ 2 now says Shamian is two metro stops away (on foot it is about 45 minutes, not 25 as the draft FAQ implied). | [OpenStreetMap Nominatim](https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q=%E9%99%88%E5%AE%B6%E7%A5%A0%20%E5%B9%BF%E5%B7%9E&format=json) |

### Canton Tower

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| **(revision)** Lighting: "the tower glows and emits light, rather than being uplit… all fixtures are located on the structure itself"; the tower is not lit from inside. The lattice "at the bottom of the tower is porous and spacious" and tightens at the waist "like a twisted rope". The innermost concrete core is painted dark grey ("最里面的混凝土核心筒采用深灰色"). Basis for "look up through the open lattice to the grey core rising inside". | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower); [Chinese Wikipedia: 广州塔](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E5%B9%BF%E5%B7%9E%E5%A1%94) |
| **(revision)** Ticket tiers (January 2025): the basic "433米白云星空观光票" covers only the 428 m and 433 m indoor halls; the 450 m outdoor platform, the bubble-cabin "摩天轮", the "极速云霄" and the 488 m platform each need a dearer ticket or package. The story names no prices. | [Guangzhou Bendibao, 2025-01-07](http://gz.bendibao.com/tour/2024719/ly351643.shtml) |
| **(revision)** Bubble cabins: 16 cars, 3.2 m across, level throughout, "taking between 20 and 40 minutes to circumnavigate the rooftop". | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canton_Tower) |
| **(revision)** The nickname now names the dancer in English too: "樱桃樊素口，杨柳小蛮腰" (Bai Juyi's singer Fansu and dancer Xiaoman). | [China News Service, 2014](https://www.chinanews.com.cn/hb/2014/07-31/6446391.shtml) |
| **(revision)** "No two angles show the same shape" is no longer attributed to the tourism bureau; the story says plainly that the outline changes with the angle (bureau: "从任意角度看都不会出现重复的形态"). | [Guangzhou culture and tourism bureau](https://wglj.gz.gov.cn/ztmb/gzhyn/ajjq/4a/content/post_8928935.html) |
| **(revision)** Season: percent of possible sunshine is lowest in March (17) and April (18), highest in October (51), November (49) and December (46). Basis for "March and April are usually the greyest months". | [Wikipedia: Guangzhou (weather box), raw text saved as rv1/gz_raw.txt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guangzhou) |
| **(revision)** Museum walk: tower (23.1090, 113.3191) to the Haixin Bridge's south end (23.1107, 113.3136) about 0.6 km, the bridge 488 m, then the north end (23.1117, 113.3133) to the Guangdong Museum (23.1174, 113.3214) about 1.0 km straight; about 2.5 km on foot, so "about half an hour's walk". | [OpenStreetMap Nominatim](https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q=%E5%B9%BF%E4%B8%9C%E7%9C%81%E5%8D%9A%E7%89%A9%E9%A6%86%20%E5%B9%BF%E5%B7%9E&format=json); [Guangzhou City Construction Investment Group](https://www.gzci.net/groupbusiness/info.aspx?itemid=282&lcid=7) |

### Shamian Island

| Fact | Source |
| --- | --- |
| **(revision)** Traffic: the 2019 renewal plan "严控外来车辆进岛", keeping parking for the White Swan, the Victory Hotel and some offices; English Wikipedia: "quiet pedestrian avenues". So "almost empty of cars", "quiet main street", 几乎不见车, 차가 거의 다니지 않는. | [Yangcheng Evening News (ycpai), 2019](https://ycpai.ycwb.com/ycppad/content/2019-03/13/content_352256.html); [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| **(revision)** Camphor leaves "smell of camphor when crushed". Basis for the fallen-leaf moment in `highlights[1]`. | [Wikipedia: Camphora officinarum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Camphora_officinarum) |
| **(revision)** White Swan: opened 6 February 1983; rated five-star by the Chinese government in February 1990, "中国大陆首批五星级宾馆之一". No rank is used. Southern Daily: "白天鹅宾馆是内地第一家向群众开放的宾馆" (not used as a rank either); the open-door story and "故乡水" photos are kept. | [Wikipedia: White Swan Hotel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_Swan_Hotel); [Chinese Wikipedia: 白天鹅宾馆](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E7%99%BD%E5%A4%A9%E9%B9%85%E5%AE%BE%E9%A6%86); [Southern Daily](https://news.southcn.com/node_8e2690394f/a15b8ea403.shtml); [Jiemian, 2019](https://www.jiemian.com/article/3010882.html) |
| **(revision)** Chapel: "Located at the French end of the island"; the French bridge and concession were at the east end. Year dropped. | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| **(revision)** Riverside: the island's south street, once "Front Avenue", runs along the river ("originally lined on the riverside by parks"). Basis for "walking the main street one way and the riverside back" (a Homeground suggestion). | [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shamian) |
| **(revision)** Night cruises: a themed route from Tianzi pier "在白鹅潭最佳观赏区域会刻意放慢航速", with "沙面建筑群" in view; the new "三江六岸" night route boards "在黄沙码头或艺术中心码头". Huangsha pier (23.109–23.111, 113.234–113.235) is just west of the island. The story says "some" cruises slow down off the island and "one route" boards at Huangsha pier. | [Guangzhou Daily, 2026-09-30](https://news.dayoo.com/guangzhou/202609/30/139995_55009028.htm); [OpenStreetMap Nominatim](https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q=%E9%BB%84%E6%B2%99%E7%A0%81%E5%A4%B4%20%E5%B9%BF%E5%B7%9E&format=json) |

## Korean names

Korean place names follow the forms used on the Korean travel market (see
`homeground-destinations-inspiration` in the project notes):

- 경산공원, 천사문대교: Trip.com KR, Tripstore.
- 톈안먼: the rest of the site uses this form.
- 위중반도, 해방비 보행거리, 양강 유람선: Trip.com KR, Tripstore, Interpark.
- 진보관, 구룡벽, 영수궁, 어화원, 낙수당: Triple and the Beijing government's Korean site.

## Font limits

- Characters outside the subset fonts were written around until the subsets are regenerated: 千厮门大桥 (厮), 紫禁城 (紫), 琉璃 (琉), 溥仪 (溥), 大禹 (禹).
- After regenerating the subsets, restore 千厮门大桥 in zh Hongyadong.
